Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on arrays structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 938. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 480 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the arrays space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 938 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 480. QED.
